let subscriptionsData = [];

function loadSubscriptions() {
  const container = document.getElementById('subscriptionsTable');

  if (container) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="loading">جاري تحميل الاشتراكات...</div>
    `;
  }

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
      try {
        subscriptionsData = data || [];
        updateSubscriptionStats(subscriptionsData);
        renderSubscriptions(subscriptionsData);
      } catch (e) {
        if (container) {
          container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="error-message">
              خطأ في عرض الاشتراكات:
              ${escapeHtml(String(e.message || e))}
            </div>
          `;
        }
        console.error(e);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      if (container) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="error-message">
            حدث خطأ أثناء تحميل الاشتراكات:
            ${escapeHtml(String(error.message || error))}
          </div>
        `;
      }
    })
    .getSubscriptions();
}

function updateSubscriptionStats(data) {

  const total = data.length;

  const active = data.filter(function(item) {
    return Number(item.remainingLessons) > 0;
  }).length;

  const finished = data.filter(function(item) {
    return Number(item.remainingLessons) === 0;
  }).length;

  const unpaid = data.filter(function(item) {
    return item.isPaid !== true;
  }).length;

  const remaining = data.reduce(function(sum, item) {
    return sum + (Number(item.remainingLessons) || 0);
  }, 0);

  function setText(id, value) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = value;
  }

  setText('subscriptionsTotal', total);
  setText('subscriptionsActive', active);
  setText('subscriptionsRemaining', remaining);
  setText('subscriptionsFinished', finished);
  setText('subscriptionsUnpaid', unpaid);
}


function renderSubscriptions(data) {

  const container =
    document.getElementById('subscriptionsTable');

  if (!container) return;

  if (!data || data.length === 0) {

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="empty-state">
        لا توجد اشتراكات حتى الآن
      </div>
    `;

    return;
  }

  let html = `
    <div class="table-wrapper">
      <table class="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>رقم الاشتراك</th>
            <th>الطالب</th>
            <th>عدد الحصص</th>
            <th>مدة الحصة</th>
            <th>سعر الساعة</th>
            <th>سعر الاشتراك</th>
            <th>تاريخ الدفع</th>
            <th>المنفذة</th>
            <th>المتبقية</th>
            <th>الحالة</th>
            <th>الإجراءات</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
  `;

  data.forEach(function(item) {


    const status = String(item.status || '');
    const isPaid = item.isPaid === true;
    const isFinished = Number(item.remainingLessons) === 0;

    let statusLabel, statusClass;
    if (isPaid) {
      statusLabel = isFinished ? 'مدفوع - منتهي' : 'مدفوع';
      statusClass = 'status-active';
    } else {
      statusLabel = isFinished ? 'منتهي - لم يُدفع' : 'لم يُدفع';
      statusClass = 'status-inactive';
    }

    let actionHtml;
    if (isPaid) {
      actionHtml = `
        <span class="status-badge status-active">✔ تم الدفع</span>
      `;
    } else {
      actionHtml = `
        <button
          class="btn btn-primary"
          onclick="paySubscription('${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}')">
          دفع
        </button>
      `;
    }

    const rowStyle =
      (!isPaid && isFinished) ? 'background:#fff4f2;' : '';

    html += `
      <tr style="${rowStyle}">
        <td>
          ${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}
        </td>

        <td>
          <strong>
            ${escapeHtml(String(item.studentName || ''))}
          </strong>
          <br>
          <small>
            ${escapeHtml(String(item.studentId || ''))}
          </small>
        </td>

        <td>${Number(item.totalLessons) || 0}</td>

        <td>${Number(item.duration) || 0} دقيقة</td>

        <td>${Number(item.hourlyRate) || 0}</td>

        <td>
          <strong>
            ${Number(item.price) || 0}
          </strong>
        </td>

        <td>
          ${escapeHtml(String(item.paymentDate || '-'))}
        </td>

        <td>
          ${Number(item.completedLessons) || 0}
        </td>

        <td>
          <strong>
            ${Number(item.remainingLessons) || 0}
          </strong>
        </td>

        <td>
          <span class="status-badge ${statusClass}">
            ${escapeHtml(statusLabel)}
          </span>
        </td>

        <td>
          ${actionHtml}
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;

  });

  html += `
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  `;

  container.innerHTML = html;
}


/*
 * تسجيل دفع الاشتراك
 */
function paySubscription(studentId) {

  if (!studentId) {
    alert('رقم الطالب غير موجود');
    return;
  }

  const confirmed = confirm(
    'هل أنت متأكد من تسجيل دفع الاشتراك لهذا الطالب؟'
  );

  if (!confirmed) return;

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {

      if (!result || !result.success) {
        alert('لم يتم تسجيل الدفع، حاول مرة أخرى');
        return;
      }

      alert('تم تسجيل دفع الاشتراك بنجاح 💰');

      loadSubscriptions();

    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {

      alert(
        'حدث خطأ أثناء تسجيل الدفع: ' +
        (error.message || error)
      );

    })
    .markSubscriptionAsPaid(studentId);

}

function filterSubscriptions() {

  const input =
    document.getElementById(
      'subscriptionSearch'
    );

  if (!input) return;

  const search =
    input.value
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (!search) {

    renderSubscriptions(
      subscriptionsData
    );

    return;
  }

  const filtered =
    subscriptionsData.filter(function(item) {

      return (

        String(item.id || '')
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(search)

        ||

        String(item.studentId || '')
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(search)

        ||

        String(item.studentName || '')
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(search)

      );

    });

  renderSubscriptions(filtered);

}


  
function convertTimeToMinutes(time) {

  if (!time) {
    return 99999;
  }


  const text =
    String(time).trim().toUpperCase();


  const match =
    text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(AM|PM)?/);


  if (!match) {
    return 99999;
  }


  let hour =
    parseInt(match[1], 10);

  const minute =
    parseInt(match[2], 10);

  const period =
    match[3];


  if (period === 'PM' && hour !== 12) {

    hour += 12;

  }


  if (period === 'AM' && hour === 12) {

    hour = 0;

  }


  return hour * 60 + minute;

}

  '😀', '😁', '😂', '🤣', '😊', '😍', '🥰', '😘',
  '😎', '🤗', '🤔', '😅', '😢', '😭', '😡', '🤩',
  '👍', '👎', '👏', '🙏', '💪', '🤝', '✌️', '👌',
  '❤️', '💚', '💙', '🌹', '⭐', '✨', '🔥', '🎉',
  '🎁', '📖', '🕌', '🤲', '🌙', '🌟', '✅', '❌',
  '⏰', '📚', '📝', '💡', '🙂', '😴', '🥳', '😇'
];

function chEl_(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

/* ---------- التحديث الدوري الخفيف + الإشعارات ---------- */

function startChatPolling_() {

  chatEnsureBell_();

  if (CHAT.pollTimer) return;

  chatPollOnce_();

  CHAT.pollTimer = setInterval(function() {
    if (document.hidden || !SESSION_TOKEN) return;
    chatPollOnce_();
  }, 20000);

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function() {
    if (!document.hidden && SESSION_TOKEN) chatPollOnce_();
  });
}

function chatEnsureBell_() {

  if (chEl_('chatBell')) return;

  const bell = document.createElement('div');
  bell.id = 'chatBell';
  bell.className = 'ch-bell';
  bell.title = 'المحادثات';
  bell.innerHTML = '💬<span class="ch-bell-badge" id="chatBellBadge"></span>';
  bell.onclick = function() { chatGoto_(null); };
  document.body.appendChild(bell);

  const toast = document.createElement('div');
  toast.id = 'chatToast';
  toast.className = 'ch-toast';
  document.body.appendChild(toast);
}

function chatGoto_(openKey) {
  CHAT.openKey = openKey || null;
  const item = document.querySelector('.menu-item[onclick^="showChat"]');
  if (item) showChat(item);
  const toast = chEl_('chatToast');
  if (toast) toast.classList.remove('show');
}

function chatSetBadge_(n) {

  const bell = chEl_('chatBell');
  const bellBadge = chEl_('chatBellBadge');
  const menuBadge = chEl_('chatMenuBadge');

  if (bell) bell.style.display = SESSION_TOKEN ? 'flex' : 'none';

  [bellBadge, menuBadge].forEach(function(b) {
    if (!b) return;
    b.textContent = n > 99 ? '99+' : String(n);
    b.style.display = n > 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
  });
}

function chatToast_(latest) {

  const toast = chEl_('chatToast');
  const bell = chEl_('chatBell');
  if (!toast) return;

  toast.innerHTML =
    '<div class="ch-toast-ico">💬</div>' +
    '<div class="ch-toast-body">' +
      '<div class="ch-toast-title">رسالة جديدة من ' +
        escapeHtml(latest.fromName || 'مستخدم') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="ch-toast-text">' + escapeHtml(latest.text || '') + '</div>' +
    '</div>';

  toast.onclick = function() { chatGoto_(latest.from); };
  toast.classList.add('show');

  if (bell) {
    bell.classList.remove('ch-ring');
    void bell.offsetWidth;
    bell.classList.add('ch-ring');
  }

  clearTimeout(chatToast_.t);
  chatToast_.t = setTimeout(function() {
    toast.classList.remove('show');
  }, 7000);
}

function chatPollOnce_() {

  if (!SESSION_TOKEN || CHAT.busy) return;

  // لو تغيّر المستخدم (تسجيل خروج ودخول بحساب آخر) نصفّر الحالة
  const uk = CURRENT_USER
    ? [CURRENT_USER.role, CURRENT_USER.linkedId, CURRENT_USER.username].join(':')
    : '';

  if (CHAT.userKey !== uk) {
    CHAT.userKey = uk;
    CHAT.ver = null;
    CHAT.unread = 0;
    CHAT.active = null;
    CHAT.contacts = [];
    chatSetBadge_(0);
  }

  CHAT.busy = true;

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {

      CHAT.busy = false;
      chatSetBadge_(CHAT.unread);   // يُظهر الجرس بعد تسجيل الدخول

      if (!res || !res.changed) return;

      CHAT.ver = res.ver;

      const prev = CHAT.unread;
      CHAT.unread = res.unread || 0;
      chatSetBadge_(CHAT.unread);

      if (chEl_('chatRoot')) {
        chatRefresh_();
      } else if (CHAT.unread > prev && res.latest) {
        chatToast_(res.latest);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function() {
      CHAT.busy = false;
    })
    .chatPoll(CHAT.ver);
}

/* ---------- الصفحة ---------- */

function showChat(element) {

  setCurrentPage('chat');
  activateMenu(element);

  document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `
    <div class="page-title">💬 المحادثات</div>

    <div class="ch-notice">
      🕒 للحفاظ على سرعة النظام، تُحذف رسائل المحادثات تلقائيًا بعد 7 أيام.
      احتفظ بأي معلومة مهمة خارج الشات.
    </div>

    <div class="ch-app" id="chatRoot">

      <aside class="ch-side">
        <div class="ch-side-head">
          <div class="ch-me" id="chMe"></div>
          <input class="ch-search" id="chSearch"
                 placeholder="🔍 ابحث عن اسم..."
                 oninput="chatSetSearch_(this.value)">
          <div class="ch-tabs" id="chTabs" style="display:none;"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="ch-list" id="chList">
          <div class="ch-noresult">جاري التحميل...</div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section class="ch-main" id="chMain">
        ${chatEmptyHtml_()}
      </section>

    </div>
  `;

  CHAT.active = null;
  CHAT.messages = [];

  chatLoadContacts_(function() {
    if (CHAT.openKey) {
      chatOpen_(CHAT.openKey);
      CHAT.openKey = null;
    }
  });
}

function chatEmptyHtml_() {
  return `
    <div class="ch-empty">
      <div class="ch-empty-ico">💬</div>
      <h3>مرحبًا بك في المحادثات</h3>
      <div>اختر محادثة من القائمة لبدء المراسلة</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

/* ---------- جهات الاتصال ---------- */

function chatLoadContacts_(callback) {

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {

      CHAT.me = res.me;
      CHAT.myName = res.myName;
      CHAT.role = res.role;
      CHAT.contacts = res.contacts || [];
      CHAT.ver = res.ver;
      CHAT.unread = res.unread || 0;
      chatSetBadge_(CHAT.unread);

      if (chEl_('chatRoot')) {

        const me = chEl_('chMe');
        if (me) me.textContent = '👤 ' + (CHAT.myName || '');

        const tabs = chEl_('chTabs');
        if (tabs) {
          if (CHAT.role === 'admin') {
            tabs.style.display = 'flex';
            chatRenderTabs_();
          } else {
            tabs.style.display = 'none';
          }
        }

        chatRenderList_();
      }

      if (callback) callback();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      const list = chEl_('chList');
      if (list) {
        list.innerHTML =
          '<div class="ch-noresult">تعذر تحميل المحادثات: ' +
          escapeHtml(String(error.message || error)) + '</div>';
      }
    })
    .getChatContacts();
}

function chatRenderTabs_() {

  const tabs = chEl_('chTabs');
  if (!tabs) return;

  const defs = [['all', 'الكل'], ['student', 'الطلاب'], ['teacher', 'المدرسون']];

  tabs.innerHTML = defs.map(function(d) {
    return '<div class="ch-tab' + (CHAT.filter === d[0] ? ' active' : '') +
      '" onclick="chatSetFilter_(\'' + d[0] + '\')">' + d[1] + '</div>';
  }).join('');
}

function chatSetFilter_(f) {
  CHAT.filter = f;
  chatRenderTabs_();
  chatRenderList_();
}

function chatSetSearch_(v) {
  CHAT.search = String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  chatRenderList_();
}

function chatRoleLabel_(role) {
  return role === 'admin' ? 'الإدارة' : (role === 'teacher' ? 'مدرس' : 'طالب');
}

function chatAvatarHtml_(c, size) {
  const letter = String(c.name || '؟').trim().charAt(0) || '؟';
  const style = size ? ' style="width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px;"' : '';
  return '<div class="ch-avatar ch-av-' + c.role + '"' + style + '>' +
    escapeHtml(letter) + '</div>';
}

function chatTimeShort_(ts) {
  if (!ts) return '';
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const now = new Date();
  if (d.toDateString() === now.toDateString()) {
    return d.toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  }
  return d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
}

function chatRenderList_() {

  const list = chEl_('chList');
  if (!list) return;

  let items = CHAT.contacts.filter(function(c) {
    if (CHAT.filter !== 'all' && c.role !== CHAT.filter) return false;
    if (CHAT.search && String(c.name).toLowerCase().indexOf(CHAT.search) === -1) return false;
    return true;
  });

  items.sort(function(a, b) {
    if ((b.lastTs || 0) !== (a.lastTs || 0)) return (b.lastTs || 0) - (a.lastTs || 0);
    return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name), 'ar');
  });

  if (!items.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="ch-noresult">لا توجد جهات اتصال</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = items.map(function(c) {

    const active = CHAT.active && CHAT.active.key === c.key;

    const last = c.lastText
      ? (c.lastMine ? 'أنت: ' : '') + c.lastText
      : 'لا توجد رسائل بعد';

    return `
      <div class="ch-item${active ? ' active' : ''}"
           data-key="${escapeHtml(c.key)}"
           onclick="chatOpen_(this.dataset.key)">

        ${chatAvatarHtml_(c)}

        <div class="ch-item-body">
          <div class="ch-item-top">
            <div class="ch-item-name">
              ${escapeHtml(c.name)}
              <span class="ch-role-chip">${chatRoleLabel_(c.role)}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="ch-item-time">${escapeHtml(chatTimeShort_(c.lastTs))}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="ch-item-bottom">
            <div class="ch-item-last">${escapeHtml(last)}</div>
            ${c.unread > 0
              ? '<div class="ch-unread">' + c.unread + '</div>'
              : ''}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

/* ---------- المحادثة ---------- */

function chatOpen_(key) {

  const contact = CHAT.contacts.find(function(c) { return c.key === key; });
  if (!contact) return;

  CHAT.active = contact;
  CHAT.messages = [];

  const app = chEl_('chatRoot');
  const main = chEl_('chMain');
  if (!app || !main) return;

  app.classList.add('ch-open');

  main.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ch-head">
      <button type="button" class="ch-back" onclick="chatBack_()">➜</button>
      ${chatAvatarHtml_(contact, 42)}
      <div>
        <div class="ch-head-name">${escapeHtml(contact.name)}</div>
        <div class="ch-head-sub">${chatRoleLabel_(contact.role)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="ch-msgs" id="chMsgs">
      <div class="ch-nomsg">جاري التحميل...</div>
    </div>

    <div class="ch-composer">

      <div class="ch-emoji-panel" id="chEmoji">
        ${CHAT_EMOJIS.map(function(e) {
          return '<span data-e="' + e + '" onclick="chatInsertEmoji_(this.dataset.e)">' + e + '</span>';
        }).join('')}
      </div>

      <button type="button" class="ch-btn-round ch-btn-emoji"
              title="إيموجي" onclick="chatToggleEmoji_()">😊</button>

      <textarea class="ch-input" id="chInput" rows="1" maxlength="1000"
                placeholder="اكتب رسالة..."
                oninput="chatAutoGrow_(this)"
                onkeydown="chatKeyDown_(event)"></textarea>

      <button type="button" class="ch-btn-round ch-btn-send"
              title="إرسال" onclick="chatSend_()">➤</button>

    </div>
  `;

  chatRenderList_();
  chatLoadMessages_(true);

  const input = chEl_('chInput');
  if (input && !('ontouchstart' in window)) input.focus();
}

function chatBack_() {
  const app = chEl_('chatRoot');
  if (app) app.classList.remove('ch-open');
  CHAT.active = null;
  chatRenderList_();
}

function chatLoadMessages_(forceBottom) {

  if (!CHAT.active) return;
  const key = CHAT.active.key;

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {

      if (!CHAT.active || CHAT.active.key !== key) return;

      // نحتفظ بالرسائل اللي لسه بتتبعت
      const local = CHAT.messages.filter(function(m) {
        return m.pending || m.failed;
      });

      CHAT.messages = (res.messages || []).concat(local);
      chatRenderMessages_(forceBottom);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      const box = chEl_('chMsgs');
      if (box) {
        box.innerHTML =
          '<div class="ch-nomsg">تعذر تحميل الرسائل: ' +
          escapeHtml(String(error.message || error)) + '</div>';
      }
    })
    .getChatMessages(key);
}

function chatRefresh_() {
  chatLoadContacts_();
  if (CHAT.active) chatLoadMessages_(false);
}

function chatDayLabel_(ts) {
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const now = new Date();
  const y = new Date();
  y.setDate(now.getDate() - 1);

  if (d.toDateString() === now.toDateString()) return 'اليوم';
  if (d.toDateString() === y.toDateString()) return 'أمس';

  return d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' });
}

function chatFormatText_(text) {
  return escapeHtml(text).replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
}

function chatRenderMessages_(forceBottom) {

  const box = chEl_('chMsgs');
  if (!box) return;

  const nearBottom =
    box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 90;

  if (!CHAT.messages.length) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="ch-nomsg">ابدأ المحادثة بإرسال رسالة 👋</div>';
    return;
  }

  let html = '';
  let lastDay = '';

  CHAT.messages.forEach(function(m) {

    const day = chatDayLabel_(m.ts);
    if (day !== lastDay) {
      html += '<div class="ch-day"><span>' + escapeHtml(day) + '</span></div>';
      lastDay = day;
    }

    const time = new Date(m.ts).toLocaleTimeString(
      'ar-EG', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }
    );

    let tick = '';
    if (m.mine) {
      tick = m.pending ? '🕓' : (m.failed ? '⚠️' : (m.read ? '✓✓' : '✓'));
    }

    html +=
      '<div class="ch-row ' + (m.mine ? 'ch-mine' : 'ch-theirs') + '">' +
        '<div class="ch-bubble' + (m.pending ? ' ch-pending' : '') +
          (m.failed ? ' ch-failed' : '') + '">' +
          chatFormatText_(m.text) +
          '<div class="ch-meta"><span>' + escapeHtml(time) + '</span>' +
            (m.mine
              ? '<span class="ch-tick' + (m.read ? ' ch-read' : '') + '">' + tick + '</span>'
              : '') +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';
  });

  box.innerHTML = html;

  if (forceBottom || nearBottom) {
    box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
  }
}

/* ---------- الإرسال والإيموجي ---------- */

function chatAutoGrow_(ta) {
  ta.style.height = 'auto';
  ta.style.height = Math.min(ta.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px';
}

function chatKeyDown_(e) {
  // الكمبيوتر: Enter يرسل و Shift+Enter سطر جديد | الموبايل: Enter سطر جديد
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !('ontouchstart' in window)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    chatSend_();
  }
}

function chatToggleEmoji_() {
  const p = chEl_('chEmoji');
  if (p) p.classList.toggle('show');
}

function chatInsertEmoji_(emoji) {
  const ta = chEl_('chInput');
  if (!ta) return;
  const s = ta.selectionStart;
  const e = ta.selectionEnd;
  ta.value = ta.value.slice(0, s) + emoji + ta.value.slice(e);
  ta.focus();
  ta.selectionStart = ta.selectionEnd = s + emoji.length;
  chatAutoGrow_(ta);
}

function chatSend_() {

  if (!CHAT.active) return;

  const ta = chEl_('chInput');
  if (!ta) return;

  const text = ta.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;

  ta.value = '';
  chatAutoGrow_(ta);

  const panel = chEl_('chEmoji');
  if (panel) panel.classList.remove('show');

  const key = CHAT.active.key;

  const temp = {
    id: 'tmp' + Date.now(),
    mine: true,
    text: text,
    ts: Date.now(),
    pending: true
  };

  CHAT.messages.push(temp);
  chatRenderMessages_(true);

  server
    .withSuccessHandler(function() {
      CHAT.messages = CHAT.messages.filter(function(m) { return m !== temp; });
      if (CHAT.active && CHAT.active.key === key) chatLoadMessages_(true);
      chatLoadContacts_();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      temp.pending = false;
      temp.failed = true;
      chatRenderMessages_(false);
      alert('تعذر إرسال الرسالة:\n' + (error.message || error));
    })
    .sendChatMessage(key, text);
}

// إغلاق لوحة الإيموجي عند الضغط خارجها
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  const panel = chEl_('chEmoji');
  if (!panel || !panel.classList.contains('show')) return;
  if (panel.contains(e.target)) return;
  if (e.target.classList && e.target.classList.contains('ch-btn-emoji')) return;
  panel.classList.remove('show');
});

            max="${currentMonth}"
          >

      <div id="payrollTable">

          جاري تحميل الرواتب...
        </div>

      </div>
